# Sombra's sounds: <sound id> = <file under E:/Overwatch Archive/extracted/154088/> (build 154088). Same format as
# ana.txt / lifeweaver.txt; build_asset_pack.py sounds copies / mixes them into the local pack. Nothing here has been
# listened to. Read 2026-11-01 offline (Overwatch and Battle.net checked closed before every archive read and DataTool /
# TankLib run): her 02C sounds' Wwise banks decoded exactly (ww.py, dump_banks.py, emote_events.py / wwfull.py from
# research/emote-sounds/scripts: children from each object's parent id, random / sequence / switch / layer parameters,
# volume, make-up gain, pitch, delays, the events' actions, RTPC curves), the Init bank's bus volumes and game parameter
# defaults (1E818, stmg.py). Scripts: E:/Overwatch Archive/research/sombra/scripts/sounds/ (sombra_gen.py writes this
# file; Ana's pipeline copied and retargeted, her scripts unchanged); their data (decode sombra_ww.json, bank dump,
# measurements, the projectile / beacon effects fx_proj.json, contact sets, voice lines, the RTPC defaults):
# research/sombra/sounds/. Effect and clip timings: research/sombra/work/fx_sounds_timed.json (fx_sounds2_sombra.py over
# work/graph_effects.json); graph nodes: research/sombra/work_kit decompiles (fx_nodes.py) and kit_*.md.
# The live kit only (her 2026-10-06 rework): Machine Pistol, Hotfix (and the hack it does on packs / deployables), Cyberspace,
# the live Translocator and the stealth it gives, EMP and the hack status. The old Hack / Virus / Opportunist, the old
# Stealth ability, the Community Crafted re-press teleport and the Stadium powers are left out ('Not mapped').
# Files: her skin and voice extraction are in research/sombra/extract, not under the build root, so those paths reach it
# through ../../research/sombra/extract/ (sombra_gen.py --canonical writes Heroes/Sombra/... and HeroVoice/Sombra/...);
# the 12 banks it lacked were extracted for this map into ExtraSounds/Sombra (extract_banks.py, DataTool's own sound
# export; manifest.json there: the shot's path crack, the shot's and Cyberspace's contacts, Cyberspace's launch, the
# beacon's landing). Ids: 02C sounds (5DCC = 000000005DCC.02C), 00D effects, 006 clips, 08F clip animation effects,
# 049 contact sets; graphs 01B with their node numbers. Confidence: H read in the data, M inferred, L guess.
# Gains: Wwise volume + make-up gain summed from each file up through its containers and mixers (the middle of a random
# range included) plus its output bus chain, plus RTPC curves (dB scaling 20*log10(1+y); linear between points) and
# SetVolume actions. RTPCs are read at their game parameter's default (the Init bank's list); modulators: an LFO at its
# middle, a time modulator at its start on one-shots and at its end on loops. As in ana.txt, ONE offset per family keeps
# her sounds' balance with each other, bringing the family's loudest single layer to -1 dBFS (the builder's limiter
# catches sums): own +4 dB (loudest: sombra.emp.pulse), 3p +1 dB (loudest: sombra.stealth.end_3p).
# Pitch: cents as a speed factor (*). Low / high-pass filters, random pitch / volume per play, fades, Seek, Wwise states
# (the -20 dB / lpf 25 'muffled' state on most mixers, group 177926770, and the others) and the voice priority RTPCs are
# not reproduced. Layers more than 60 dB down are left out, and in loops those 20 dB under the loudest loop. A loop's
# LOOP LENGTH is its longest bed within 6 dB of the loudest layer; shorter layers are tiled across it and beds more than
# 15 % longer are left out.
# Own vs others: her STUSounds with audience 1 (5DCC, 3B42, 4AEC, 3BD0, 5DC9, 5DCA, 3B24, 4AF3, 3B20, 3B85, 3879, 3871,
# 5DC7) are read as hers (H by Lucio's pairs: audience 1 was his own, 2 the others'); 3B20 and 3B85 carry a range
# because they sound at the beacon. The other no-range copies (audience 2) are read as the player the effect plays on
# (M, Ana's and Lifeweaver's reading): 'own' is Sombra, or the healed ally / hacked target / weakened enemy where a rule
# says. Ranged copies are everyone else's ('3p'); where an effect holds two ranged copies with the same containers they
# are read as the two teams' copies (L which): the louder is built, the other noted; copies with different files all
# play (built together, M). Projectile, zone and contact sounds with no own copy are built once for everyone ('both').
# Ranges come through the volume (sounds.md). Her automatic pistol's Wwise containers re-trigger at the fire rate (20 a
# second) while she fires: built as one shot's worth for the game to play per shot, the burst's tail apart (fire_end).

# ===== How each game event is put together (rules)
# sombra.pistol.fire: shot, own: 02C 5DCC of 1999 (no range, audience 1: hers, H): a 0.15 s report, a random 17 of
#  0.10 s cracks at +400 cents, a 0.63 s body at +200, a random 22 of 0.14 s and a random 5 of 0.11 s at -280 cents.
#  5DCC's containers are continuous trigger-rate random containers paced by the fire-rate parameter 4066808683 (0.05 s
#  at its default 100 = 20 a second, H): Overwatch keeps re-triggering them while she fires. Built here as one trigger
#  of each (one shot's worth), for the game to play per shot (M); the 2.5-3.1 s tail container (in Switch 473299206,
#  default case) is left to fire_end, since a tail per shot would pile up 60 tails (M).
# Trigger: each shot (PRIMARY held, 20 a second; 0B1B volley #94 makes the hitscan entity 1222, whose m_effect 1998
#  holds sub-effect 1999 with 5DCC / 680F on the muzzle hp 0006).
# Layers: a 5DCC 999770480 RanSeq 0.14 s x22, net -13.5; b 5DCC 550514449 RanSeq 0.10-0.11 s x17 +400 cents, net
#  -32.5; c 5DCC 689072370 RanSeq 0.11 s x5 -280 cents, net -13; d 5DCC 560866164 RanSeq 0.63 s +200 cents, net -21; e
#  5DCC 465891818 Sound 0.15 s, net -8.5.
@sombra.pistol.fire = sombra.pistol.fire.a@0.025:-9.5 + sombra.pistol.fire.b*1.260@0.025:-28.5 + sombra.pistol.fire.c*0.851@0.025:-9 + sombra.pistol.fire.d*1.122:-17 + sombra.pistol.fire.e:-4.5
# sombra.pistol.fire_end: burst tail, own: 5DCC's tail container (a random 10 of 2.5-3.1 s at -136 cents, pitch RTPCs
#  at their ends) and its Stop-event click (-350 cents).
# Trigger: the burst ends (PRIMARY released, the clip empty, an ability): 5DCC's Stop event breaks the tail container
#  (it rings out) and plays a 0.05 s click (H); M that the game's per-shot effect ending is this moment.
# Layers: a 5DCC 165278408 RanSeq 2.54-3.06 s x10 -136 cents, net -11.5; b 5DCC Stop-event 859443435 Sound 0.05 s -350
#  cents, net -16.5.
@sombra.pistol.fire_end = sombra.pistol.fire_end.a*0.924@0.025:-7.5 + sombra.pistol.fire_end.b*0.817@0.03:-12.5
# sombra.pistol.fire_3p: shot heard by others: 02C 680F of 1999 (200 m, audience 2): the 0.15 s report, a random 16 of
#  0.17-0.19 s, the random 22 of 0.14 s and the random 5 of 0.11 s, built per shot as fire is (M).
# Trigger: the same shot, at her for everyone else (200 m).
# Layers: a 680F 766467227 RanSeq 0.14 s x22, net -18; b 680F 717157735 RanSeq 0.17-0.19 s x16, net -26; c 680F
#  663577396 RanSeq 0.11 s x5 -100 cents, net -17.5; d 680F 381351833 Sound 0.15 s, net -17.
@sombra.pistol.fire_3p = sombra.pistol.fire_3p.a@0.01:-17 + sombra.pistol.fire_3p.b@0.05:-25 + sombra.pistol.fire_3p.c*0.944@0.005:-16.5 + sombra.pistol.fire_3p.d:-16
# sombra.pistol.fire_end_3p: burst tail heard by others: 680F's tail container (a random 10 of 2.5-3.1 s at -136 cents).
# Trigger: the same burst end, at her for everyone else (680F's Stop event plays no sound of its own, H).
# Layers: a 680F 88215297 RanSeq 2.54-3.06 s x10 -136 cents, net -19.
@sombra.pistol.fire_end_3p = sombra.pistol.fire_end_3p.a*0.924:-18
# sombra.pistol.shot: shot crack along its path: 02C 1801 of 008C (60 m: a random 35 of 0.36-2.47 s at -45 cents and a
#  random 28 of 0.42-1.15 s at -375). 0F98 is the same containers 1 dB quieter (the other team's, L which; the louder
#  built). Not in her extraction (ExtraSounds/Sombra/PistolShot).
# Trigger: each shot: the hitscan entity 1222's m_2170A015 'sound only' effect 008C (two Sound components on hp 00A4:
#  0F98 and 1801), as the shot is made; M that it sounds along the shot's path (1222 has no speed).
# Layers: a 1801 439357212 RanSeq 0.36-2.47 s x35 -45 cents, net -22; b 1801 259271031 RanSeq 0.42-1.15 s x28 -375
#  cents, net -14.5.
@sombra.pistol.shot = sombra.pistol.shot.a*0.974@0.003:-21 + sombra.pistol.shot.b*0.805@0.005:-13.5
# sombra.pistol.hit: hit confirm, own: 02C 8DE7 (no range, on the weapon bus: the shooter's crack shared with Ana and
#  Kiriko): random sets at +710 / +430 / +535 cents.
# Trigger: a shot hits a character: 1999's sub-effect 4010 (on the muzzle hp 0006) plays 8DE7, the sub-effect Ana's
#  dart hit and Kiriko's kunai play as the hit confirmation; M that it sounds only on a hit (1999 is the shot's own
#  effect).
# Layers: a 8DE7 413748272 RanSeq 0.41-0.61 s x16 +430 cents, net -22; b 8DE7 19863385 RanSeq 0.54-0.60 s x10 +710
#  cents, net -12; c 8DE7 1057399041 RanSeq 0.49-0.56 s x7 +535 cents, net -18.5.
@sombra.pistol.hit = sombra.pistol.hit.a*1.282@0.002:-18 + sombra.pistol.hit.b*1.507@0.002:-8 + sombra.pistol.hit.c*1.362@0.002:-14.5
# sombra.pistol.hit_block: shot hit on the world: 02C 849E (90 m) + 84CB (32 m): a random 22 of 0.15-0.27 s and a
#  random 28 of 0.45-1.32 s (H). The other 22 materials have their own pairs (contacts.json). Not in her extraction
#  (ExtraSounds/Sombra/PistolContact).
# Trigger: a shot hits a block: 1222's contact set 05A7, contact type 2, default material 0001 -> contact effect 20AD
#  -> 849E + 84CB (both play), at the hit point.
# Layers: a 84CB 674937543 RanSeq 0.45-1.32 s x29 +55 cents, net -26; b 849E 941752556 RanSeq 0.45-1.32 s x28 -125
#  cents, net -16; c 849E 153573411 RanSeq 0.15-0.27 s x22, net +1.5; d 84CB 214250824 RanSeq 0.15-0.27 s x22, net
#  -7.5.
@sombra.pistol.hit_block = sombra.pistol.hit_block.a*1.032@0.02:-25 + sombra.pistol.hit_block.b*0.930@0.045:-15 + sombra.pistol.hit_block.c:+2.5 + sombra.pistol.hit_block.d:-6.5

@sombra.pistol.draw = sombra.pistol.draw.a*1.414:-18.5 + sombra.pistol.draw.b*2.000@0.25:-33.5

# sombra.reload: reload, own: 02C 9228 (0.34 s), 3BD4 (0.05 s), 9229 (0.75 s), 922A (0.43 s), 4361 (a random 5 of
#  0.12-0.14 s and a 0.03 s click), all no range (H), at their clip times.

#  plays anim alias 0x004 over 1.2 s; the 1P clip 3D4A (1.367 s, effect 98D0) plays 9228 @0.0373, 3BD4 @0.1, 9229
#  @0.3907, 922A @1.036, 4361 @1.3 (H), here scaled by 1.2 / 1.367 (M: the clip is fitted to the reload's 1.2 s).

#  846584611 Sound 0.05 s, net -7.5; d 9229 978249858 Sound 0.75 s, net -16.5; e 922A 53855002 Sound 0.43 s, net

# sombra.hotfix.target.loop: target found, own: 02C 3B42 of 2C75 (no range, audience 1: an endless 14.69 s Sound).
# Trigger: while Hotfix is held and its cone finds a target: 614C #2597 StateTrackTargets holds #2599 2C75 on her (M:
#  the lock-on hum).
# Layers: a 3B42 717055856 Sound 14.69 s, net -11, loops every 14.685 s.
# LOOP LENGTH 14.685 s: play it again every LOOP LENGTH (the built file runs 14.69 s: shorter layers are tiled to fill
# the loop and the last tile runs over; Overwatch's layers loop on their own lengths, which one file cannot do).
@sombra.hotfix.target.loop = sombra.hotfix.target.loop.a:-7
# sombra.hotfix.target.end: target lost, own: 3B42's Stop event (a random 4 of 0.23-0.38 s at +330 cents and a random
#  2 of 0.18-0.22 s at +640).

# sombra.hotfix.end_3p: Hotfix lowered heard by others: 02C 3C3A (70 m: the same 0.42 s file and a 0.50 s one at +300
#  cents 0.2 s late).

# ===== For the integration: each event, loop or one-shot, who it is built for, its length, and what triggers it
# (own = the player the effect plays on, in the head: Sombra, or the target where the rule says; 3p = everyone else, at her
# / the target / the hit point; both = one rule for everyone). 'EXTRA' = in the data but not needed for the kit's
# moments. Not built: see 'Not mapped'.
#   sombra.pistol.fire                 one-shot own  0.56 s; each shot (PRIMARY held, 20 a second; 0B1B volley #94
#     makes the hitscan entity 1222, whose m_effect 1998 holds sub-effect 1999 with 5DCC / 680F on the muzzle hp 0006)
#   sombra.pistol.fire_end             one-shot own  3.33 s; the burst ends (PRIMARY released, the clip empty, an
#     ability): 5DCC's Stop event breaks the tail container (it rings out) and plays a 0.05 s click (H); M that the
#     game's per-shot effect ending is this moment
#   sombra.pistol.fire_3p              one-shot 3p   0.24 s; the same shot, at her for everyone else (200 m)
#   sombra.pistol.fire_end_3p          one-shot 3p   3.30 s; the same burst end, at her for everyone else (680F's Stop
#     event plays no sound of its own, H)
#   sombra.pistol.shot                 one-shot both 2.54 s; each shot: the hitscan entity 1222's m_2170A015 'sound
#     only' effect 008C (two Sound components on hp 00A4: 0F98 and 1801), as the shot is made; M that it sounds along
#     the shot's path (1222 has no speed)
#   sombra.pistol.hit                  one-shot own  0.48 s; a shot hits a character: 1999's sub-effect 4010 (on the
#     muzzle hp 0006) plays 8DE7, the sub-effect Ana's dart hit and Kiriko's kunai play as the hit confirmation; M
#     that it sounds only on a hit (1999 is the shot's own effect)
#   sombra.pistol.hit_block            one-shot both 1.47 s; a shot hits a block: 1222's contact set 05A7, contact
#     type 2, default material 0001 -> contact effect 20AD -> 849E + 84CB (both play), at the hit point
#   sombra.pistol.draw                 one-shot own  0.67 s; the pistol is put in her hand: 0B1B #73
#     StateCosmeticEntity (pistol 1221 on hp 000A) onBegin -> #183 ActionEffect 0B8C on hp 001D (M: when the weapon
#     state begins: spawn, hero pick)
#   sombra.pistol.draw_3p              one-shot 3p   0.67 s; the same, at her for everyone else (45 m)
#   sombra.reload                      one-shot own  1.34 s; reload (R with the clip not full, or automatic at 0
#     rounds; 1.2 s, the clip refilled at 0.48 s): 0B1B #141 plays anim alias 0x004 over 1.2 s; the 1P clip 3D4A
#     (1.367 s, effect 98D0) plays 9228 @0.0373, 3BD4 @0.1, 9229 @0.3907, 922A @1.036, 4361 @1.3 (H), here scaled by
#     1.2 / 1.367 (M: the clip is fitted to the reload's 1.2 s)
#   sombra.reload_3p                   one-shot 3p   1.33 s; the same reload, at her for everyone else: the 3P clip
#     3CD8 (1.5 s, effect 1E1C) plays 9636 @0, 9637 @0.4545, 9638 @1.1296 (H), scaled by 1.2 / 1.5 (M)
#   sombra.hotfix.target.loop          LOOP     own  loop 14.685 s; while Hotfix is held and its cone finds a target:
#     614C #2597 StateTrackTargets holds #2599 2C75 on her (M: the lock-on hum)
#   sombra.hotfix.target.end           one-shot own  0.31 s; the cone loses its target or Hotfix ends: 2C75 stops,
#     which runs 3B42's Stop event (it stops the hum and plays two blips)
#   sombra.hotfix.cast                 one-shot own  0.63 s; the 0.5 s channel starts (SECONDARY held on a target):
#     the 1P Hotfix alias 0x47D plays 3E22 (0.167 s raise) then, hacking, 1B379 (0.5 s, effect 1E08) with 4AEC at 0 (H
#     clip; M that it starts 0.167 s after the press)
#   sombra.hotfix.cast_3p              one-shot 3p   0.63 s; the same cast, at her for everyone else: the 3P upper
#     layer's clip 3EA5 (0.5 s, effect 1EB1) plays 3B43 at 0 (H clip; M that it is the cast's start)
#   sombra.hotfix.channel              one-shot own  1.03 s; during the 0.5 s channel on an ally hero (v75856 true):
#     614C #1405 StateWait (0.5 s) -> #2191 -> #2197 holds #2779 7DF5 (on the ally, v8909) and #2783 7DF6 (on her hp
#     0002); stop it when the channel ends
#   sombra.hotfix.channel_ally         one-shot own  1.00 s; the same channel, what the ally hears (#2779 7DF5 on the
#     ally); stop it when the channel ends
#   sombra.hotfix.channel_3p           one-shot 3p   1.94 s; the same channel, at her hand for everyone else: #2783
#     7DF6 on her hp 0002 holds both (50 m); stop it when the channel ends
#   sombra.hack.channel                one-shot own  1.94 s; during the 0.5 s channel on anything else (a health pack,
#     an enemy deployable: v75856 false): #2191 -> #2198 holds #2190 1AC7 (on her, beam from hp 0021 to the target's
#     0001); stop it when the channel ends
#   sombra.hack.channel_3p             one-shot 3p   1.94 s; the same channel, at her for everyone else (50 m)
#   sombra.hack.channel_enemy (EXTRA)  one-shot 3p   2.34 s; EXTRA: the same channel, 1AC7's other ranged copy (50 m)
#   sombra.hotfix.end                  one-shot own  0.42 s; the channel ends and the 0.3 s recovery plays anim alias
#     0x47D's recovery clips (614C #261 / #419 / #420 / #423): the 1P 3E23 / 3E50 / 4006 (effects 1E07 / 1E80 / 1ED7)
#     play 3BD0 at 0 on hp 0002 (H clips; M which category is which ending)
